▶ the Strands AgentExecutor satisfies the same semantic contract
  ✔ one model tool use becomes a semantic operation call, before any native execution (14.983458ms)
  ✔ the continuation is a JSON snapshot that survives a round trip (1.409834ms)
  ✔ resuming with an observation feeds the model, and never re-runs the operation (57.516583ms)
  ✔ a denial is reported to the model as faithfully as a success (10.092292ms)
  ✔ several tool uses in one turn are captured together (1.192042ms)
  ✔ cancellation propagates where the framework supports it (0.549166ms)
  ✔ no callback in this package can reach the gateway, a dispatcher, or a settlement path (12.797291ms)
  ✔ the bridge forwards the projected observation and shapes no semantics of its own (24.396916ms)
  ✔ the model bridge builds its specs from the projection, not from the framework registry (0.672083ms)
  ✔ driven by the real AgentController, no operation reaches an executor without the Harness (76.776042ms)

  ✔ strict import turns a refusal into a startup error instead of a quiet absence (0.326ms)
✔ imported MCP identity is chosen locally (7.173709ms)

  ✔ annotations never reach the descriptor the exposure and dispatch path reads (0.173708ms)

✔ remote metadata is information, never permission (1.259916ms)

  ✔ the supported subset round-trips with the same acceptance set (20.564458ms)

  ✔ nested object defaults are normalized independently (0.121209ms)
  ✔ composition keywords are refused, never flattened (0.146625ms)

  ✔ an unrecognized dialect is refused; a known one and pure annotations are accepted (0.098458ms)
  ✔ an unknown keyword refuses by default rather than being dropped (0.062667ms)
